/* Hide excerpt mobile */


/* move search nav mag glass */

.nav .searchform .searchsubmit {

    padding: 18px 5px 10px 15px;
}

#mega-menu-wrap-main_menu #mega-menu-main_menu>li.mega-menu-item.mega-current-menu-ancestor>a.mega-menu-link {
    background: #247090;
    color: #ffffff;

    text-decoration: none;
    border-color: #fff;
}

.nav .searchform input {
    border: 2px solid #247090;
}

.section-title, #comments h3, #comments .widget-title {
    border-bottom: 2px solid #000000;
}


.nav {
    height: 60px;
background: #ffffff;
}

/* hide comments */

.entry-submeta span:nth-child(2) {
    display: none;
}

.entry-submeta>span:after{
display:none;	
}

/* padding below header */
.head-wrap {
    padding-bottom: 0px;
   }

/* time on slides - move 5px, add background */
.page-id-88 time.item-time {
    padding-left: 5px;
background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35);
border-top-left-radius: 8px!important;
    border-top-right-radius: 8px!important;
    border-bottom-right-radius: 8px!important;
    border-bottom-left-radius: 8px!important;
padding-top: 2px!important;
    padding-bottom: 2px!important;
   
    border-top-width: 1px!important;

   }

div.item-categories
{
    display: none;
   }

/* center gallery slider */
.widget-post-slider .wps-caption
{
    text-align: center;
}

/* background behind slide title */
.item-md .item-title {
    background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.35)
   }
/* fix slider on ipad */
@media(max-width:991px) {
    .slider > .slider-left {
        width: 100%;
    }
    .slider >.slider-right {
        width: 100%;
    }
}
.slider:after {
    clear: both!important;
}

/* centre logo */

.head-wrap-col-left {
    margin: auto;
    text-align: center;
    width: 100%;
}
.head-wrap-col-right {
    display: none;
}

/* clickable images */
.slider article {cursor:pointer;}

.slider  div.col-md-3 div.item-content {
    margin-bottom: -10px;
   }

.slider  div.col-md-6 div.item-content {
    margin-bottom: -10px;
   }
div.col-xs-12
{
    margin-bottom: -10px;
   }

/* hide featured image text from widget */
div.entry-related
div.ccfic {     
  display:none;
}

/* hide black footer bar */
div.foot {     
  display:none;
}

/* hide page titles */
div.page-title {     
  display:none;
}

.slider >div{
    height: 100%;
    position:relative;
}
/* fix layout2 */
@media (max-width: 991px) {
    .slider >div {
        height: 100%;
        position: relative;
        clear: left;
        float: left;
        width: 100%;
    }
    .slider .slider-half {
        margin: 20px 0;
    }
    .slider .col-md-3:first-child {
       
        display: none;
      
    }
}


.item-media .item-thumb.full{
 width:100%!important;   
    position:relative;
}
.item-media .item-thumb.full:before{
content: "";
    position: absolute;
    bottom: 0;
    height: 53%;
    left: 0;
    width: 100%;
    background: linear-gradient(to bottom, transparent 0%, transparent 20%, rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.7) 100%);
}
.item-content.absoluted{
    position:absolute;
    bottom:0;
    top:auto;
    padding:20px;
    
}
.item-content.absoluted a,.item-content.absoluted{
 color:#fff;   
}

.item-thumb.full{
max-height:190px;
    overflow:hidden;
}
.section-category .col-sm-6:nth-of-type(2n+1){
 clear:left;   
}
.section-category .col-sm-6{
 min-height:220px!important;
}
.item-content.smaller {
    font-size: 14px;
    font-family: sans-serif;
    line-height: 15px;
    padding-left:5px;
    max-height: 32px;
    overflow: hidden;
	width:90%;  
}

.item-content.absoluted{
 width:100%;   
}

/* CSS REWRITE */

/* post title */
.entry-title {
    font-family: 'Droid Serif'!important;
    font-size: 36px!important;
    font-style: normal!important;
    font-weight: 700!important;
}

/* search button */
.nav .searchform .searchsubmit {
    color: #fff;
    font-size: 30px;
    padding-top: 1px;
}
/* item title on listings */
.item-title {
    display: inline-block!important;
    font-family: 'Droid Serif'!important;
    font-size: 18px!important;
    font-style: normal!important;
    font-weight: 700!important;
    border-top-left-radius: 8px!important;
    border-top-right-radius: 8px!important;
    border-bottom-right-radius: 8px!important;
    border-bottom-left-radius: 8px!important;
}

/* gallery slider text */

    font-family: 'Droid Serif';
    font-size: 15px;
    font-style: normal;
    font-weight: 700;
    text-transform: none;
}

/* REMOVAL OF DROPDOWN ARROWS ON MENU*/
#mega-menu-wrap-main_menu #mega-menu-main_menu li.mega-menu-item-has-children > a.mega-menu-link:after {
display: none;
    content: '\f140';
    font-family: dashicons;
    margin: 0 0 0 6px;
    vertical-align: top;
    -webkit-font-smoothing: antialiased;
    -moz-osx-font-smoothing: grayscale;
    transform: rotate(0);
    color: inherit;
}

div.rp4wp-related-posts.rp4wp-related-post span.ccfic-text{
    display: none;
}

    display: none;
}

color: #000;
font-weight: bold;

  font-size: 14px;

}

  padding-bottom: 0px;
  margin-bottom: 0px;
}

    display: inline-block;

    padding: 0px; 
    padding-right: 6px;

}

}

font-weight: 700;

    font-weight: 700!important;}

div.emd-container .excerpt {
    padding: 0px;
display: none;
}

    display: none;
}

.video-grid-standard-title {
font-family: 'Droid Serif'!important;
font-weight: 700!important;
    padding: 10px;
    font-size: .9rem;
    overflow: hidden;
    height: 55px;
}

figcaption.video-grid-standard-title {
    font-family: 'Roboto'!important;
    font-weight: 700!important;
    padding: 0px;
    font-size: .7rem;
    overflow: hidden;
    height: 55px;
}

/* MAKE CAPTION TEXT FOR IN POST IMAGES SAME AS FEAT IMAGE*/
.wp-caption .wp-caption-text {

    font-size: 13px;
    line-height: 17px;
    margin: 3px 0 5px;
    padding: 5px 0 0;

    font-style: normal;
font-weight: 400!important;
}

/* READ MORE TAGS AFTER SIDEBAR CATEGORY DISPLAYS*/
.cat-post-footer-link {
    background-color: #247090;
color: #ffffff;
    font-family: 'Droid Serif';
    font-size: 18px;
    font-style: normal;
    font-weight: 700;
    margin-left: 20px;
    padding-top: 6px;
    padding-bottom: 6px;
    padding-left: 6px;
    padding-right: 6px;
    border-top-left-radius: 4px;
    border-top-right-radius: 4px;
    border-bottom-right-radius: 4px;
    border-bottom-left-radius: 4px;

/* SHARE ICONS AT BOTTOM OF POST*/
.sfsi_Sicons {
  padding-bottom: 24px;
  font-weight: 700;

font-size: 18px;
}


/* POST SLIDER SIZE CSS/
.recent-post-slider .post-image-bg img {
    height: auto !important;
    width: 100% !important;
    object-fit: unset !important;
   }


/* REMOVAL OF NATIVE SEARCH*/



/*  SEARCH BOX*/
#ajaxsearchlite1 .probox .proinput input, div.asl_w .probox .proinput input {
    font-weight: 300;
    color: #fff!important;
    font-size: 16px;
font-style: italic;
    line-height: normal!important;
    text-shadow: 0 0 0 rgba(255,255,255,0);
    border: 0;
    box-shadow: none;
    height: 34px;
}

/* NAV ARROW*/
div.asl_m .probox .promagnifier .innericon svg, div.asl_m .probox .prosettings .innericon svg, div.asl_m .probox .proloading svg {
    height: 120%;
    width: 22px;
    vertical-align: baseline;
    display: inline-block;
}

/* SEARCH BOX BACKGROUND*/

#mega-menu-wrap-main_menu #mega-menu-main_menu > li.mega-menu-item.mega-toggle-on > a.mega-menu-link, #mega-menu-wrap-main_menu #mega-menu-main_menu > li.mega-menu-item > a.mega-menu-link:hover, #mega-menu-wrap-main_menu #mega-menu-main_menu > li.mega-menu-item > a.mega-menu-link:focus {
    background: #247090;
    color: #fff;
    font-weight: bold;
    text-decoration: none;
    border-color: #fff;
}

div.asl_r.vertical {
    padding: 4px;
    background: #247090;
    border-radius: 3px;
    border: 0 none #000;
    border-radius: 0 0 0 0;
    box-shadow: 0 0 0 0 #000;
    visibility: hidden;
    display: none;
}

/* EXIT POPUP*/
#exitpopup-modal h3 {
    color: #fff;
    font-size: 22px;
  font-family: 'Droid Serif';
    margin: 0.2em;
    text-transform: none;
    font-weight: 700;
}

#exitpopup-modal .modal-body {
    padding: 20px 35px;
    font-size: 18px;
   font-family: 'Droid Serif';
}

/* FOOTER AFTER AUTOPTIMISE*/
.footer {
    padding-top: 0px;
}

cannot solve</a><p class="cpwp-excerpt-text">Years after the pandemic, a post-apocalyptic film about being confined in one place might seem redundant, but The Last House is constructed on family values and culture.</p></div></li><li class="cat-post-item"><div><a class="cat-post-thumbnail cat-post-none" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/502002098/the-end-of-oak-street-inside-the-worlds-most-dangerous-street/" title="‘The End of Oak Street’:  Inside the world’s most dangerous street"><span class="cat-post-crop cat-post-format cat-post-format-standard"><img class="attachment-large size-large wp-post-image br-lazy" src="data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB4bWxucz0naHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmcnIHZpZXdCb3g9JzAgMCAzNDAgMjAwJz48L3N2Zz4=" data-breeze="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/126727.jpg" width="340" height="200"   alt="" data-cat-posts-width="340" data-cat-posts-height="200" loading="lazy" /></span></a></div><div> <a class="cat-post-title" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/502002098/the-end-of-oak-street-inside-the-worlds-most-dangerous-street/" rel="bookmark">‘The End of Oak Street’:  Inside the world’s most dangerous street</a><p class="cpwp-excerpt-text">Step into a world where the familiar becomes terrifying. Now showing in cinemas, The End of Oak Street brings a thrilling sci-fi survival adventure combining action, mystery, family drama, and untamed danger.</p></div></li><li class="cat-post-item"><div><a class="cat-post-thumbnail cat-post-none" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501999135/how-phnom-penhs-old-buildings-preserve-the-citys-heritage/" title="How Phnom Penh’s old buildings preserve the city’s heritage"><span class="cat-post-crop cat-post-format cat-post-format-standard"><img class="attachment-large size-large wp-post-image br-lazy" src="data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB4bWxucz0naHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmcnIHZpZXdCb3g9JzAgMCAzNDAgMjAwJz48L3N2Zz4=" data-breeze="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/126686.jpg" width="340" height="200"   alt="" data-cat-posts-width="340" data-cat-posts-height="200" loading="lazy" /></span></a></div><div> <a class="cat-post-title" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501999135/how-phnom-penhs-old-buildings-preserve-the-citys-heritage/" rel="bookmark">How Phnom Penh’s old buildings preserve the city’s heritage</a><p class="cpwp-excerpt-text">Phnom Penh is changing quickly. Apartments, shopping centres, cafés and office buildings are sprouting up across the city, yet among the construction are old buildings that have stood for decades. Some are easy to notice, while others are hidden behind new signs and businesses.</p></div></li><li class="cat-post-item"><div><a class="cat-post-thumbnail cat-post-none" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501999131/unearthing-preah-khset-the-forgotten-angkorian-jewel-of-srey-snam/" title="Unearthing Preah Khset, the forgotten Angkorian jewel of Srey snam"><span class="cat-post-crop cat-post-format cat-post-format-standard"><img class="attachment-large size-large wp-post-image br-lazy" src="data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB4bWxucz0naHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmcnIHZpZXdCb3g9JzAgMCAzNDAgMjAwJz48L3N2Zz4=" data-breeze="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/126663.jpg" width="340" height="200"   alt="" data-cat-posts-width="340" data-cat-posts-height="200" loading="lazy" /></span></a></div><div> <a class="cat-post-title" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501999131/unearthing-preah-khset-the-forgotten-angkorian-jewel-of-srey-snam/" rel="bookmark">Unearthing Preah Khset, the forgotten Angkorian jewel of Srey snam</a><p class="cpwp-excerpt-text">Off National Road 68, a journey into the countryside of Siem Reap province’s Srey Snam district leads to Preah Khset, a remote Angkorian temple ruin that offers visitors a glimpse into Cambodia’s ancient heritage and rural landscape.</p></div></li><li class="cat-post-item"><div><a class="cat-post-thumbnail cat-post-none" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501992685/from-phnom-penh-to-battambang-on-two-wheels/" title="From Phnom Penh to Battambang on two wheels"><span class="cat-post-crop cat-post-format cat-post-format-standard"><img class="attachment-large size-large wp-post-image br-lazy" src="data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB4bWxucz0naHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmcnIHZpZXdCb3g9JzAgMCAzNDAgMjAwJz48L3N2Zz4=" data-breeze="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/126457.jpg" width="340" height="200"   alt="" data-cat-posts-width="340" data-cat-posts-height="200" loading="lazy" /></span></a></div><div> <a class="cat-post-title" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501992685/from-phnom-penh-to-battambang-on-two-wheels/" rel="bookmark">From Phnom Penh to Battambang on two wheels</a><p class="cpwp-excerpt-text">If you want to experience Cambodia by motorbike, the 291-kilometre journey from Phnom Penh to Battambang is well worth the trip. Following National Road 5, one of the country’s best-paved highways, the ride offers a chance to experience rural landscapes, riverside communities, historic sites, and everyday life along the way.</p></div></li></ul></aside><aside id="sow-editor-10" class="extendedwopts-show extendedwopts-desktop extendedwopts-tablet extendedwopts-mobile widget group widget_sow-editor"><div

/></span></a></div><div> <a class="cat-post-title" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501992697/how-the-ai-revolution-is-coding-cambodias-next-era/" rel="bookmark">How the AI revolution is coding Cambodia’s next era</a><p class="cpwp-excerpt-text">Cambodia is entering a new stage of digital transformation, with government institutions, businesses and educational organisations showing growing interest in how artificial intelligence (AI) can contribute to the Kingdom’s future development.</p></div></li><li class="cat-post-item"><div><a class="cat-post-thumbnail cat-post-none" src="data:image/svg+xml;base64,PHN2ZyB4bWxucz0naHR0cDovL3d3dy53My5vcmcvMjAwMC9zdmcnIHZpZXdCb3g9JzAgMCAzNDAgMjAwJz48L3N2Zz4=" data-breeze="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/wp-content/uploads/2026/08/126043.jpg" width="340" height="200"   alt="" data-cat-posts-width="340" data-cat-posts-height="200" loading="lazy" /></span></a></div><div> <a class="cat-post-title" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501980357/how-sustainability-drives-cambodias-business-growth/" rel="bookmark">How sustainability drives Cambodia’s business growth</a><p class="cpwp-excerpt-text">As Cambodia’s economy becomes increasingly connected to regional and global markets, businesses are facing rising expectations to operate responsibly, sustainably and transparently. Beyond financial performance, companies are now being assessed on how they manage their environmental, social and governance responsibilities.</p></div></li><li class="cat-post-item"><div><a class="cat-post-thumbnail cat-post-none" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501950612/grounded-growth-whats-blocking-cambodias-tourism-recovery/" title="Grounded growth: What’s blocking Cambodia’s blocking Cambodia’s tourism recovery?</a><p class="cpwp-excerpt-text">Going by the latest statistics, the overall scenario of the tourism sector presents a grim outlook with international arrivals falling 47.9 percent to 1.75 million in the first half of 2026, down from 3.36 million a year earlier.</p></div></li><li class="cat-post-item"><div><a class="cat-post-thumbnail cat-post-none" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501946164/can-cambodias-new-reform-push-secure-its-place-in-global-economy/" title="Can Cambodia’s new reform push secure its place in global economy?"><span class="cat-post-crop cat-post-format cat-post-format-standard"><img class="attachment-large size-large wp-post-image br-lazy" /></span></a></div><div> <a class="cat-post-title" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501946164/can-cambodias-new-reform-push-secure-its-place-in-global-economy/" rel="bookmark">Can Cambodia’s new reform push secure its place in global economy?</a><p class="cpwp-excerpt-text">As the global economy navigates an increasingly complex landscape marked by geopolitical tensions, trade fragmentation, shifting supply chains and slowing growth, Cambodia is accelerating an ambitious reform agenda aimed at reinforcing investor confidence and positioning the Kingdom as one of the most competitive investment destinations in the region.</p></div></li><li class="cat-post-item"><div><a class="cat-post-thumbnail cat-post-none" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501941602/post-ldc-cambodia-aspires-to-become-self-reliant-and-competitive/" title="Post-LDC, Cambodia aspires to become self-reliant and competitive"><span /></span></a></div><div> <a class="cat-post-title" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501941602/post-ldc-cambodia-aspires-to-become-self-reliant-and-competitive/" rel="bookmark">Post-LDC, Cambodia aspires to become self-reliant and competitive</a><p class="cpwp-excerpt-text">Come December 2029, a new chapter shall make way into the annals of Cambodian history when the country graduates from the Least Developed Country (LDC) status.</p></div></li><li class="cat-post-item"><div><a class="cat-post-thumbnail cat-post-none" href="https://www.khmertimeskh.com/501934590/what-should-cambodia-do-to-revive-tourism-amid-sharp-drop-in-visitors/" title="What should Cambodia do to revive tourism amid sharp drop in Cambodia do to revive tourism amid sharp drop in visitors?</a><p class="cpwp-excerpt-text">While Cambodia reported a significant rise in domestic tourist activities during the first five months of the year, the slump in international arrivals has raised concerns among the stakeholders.</p></div></li><li class="cat-post-item"><div><a class="cat-post-thumbnail cat-post-none" 				var cat_posts_namespace              = window.cat_posts_namespace || {};

				cat_posts_namespace.layout_img_size  = cat_posts_namespace.layout_img_size || {};

				cat_posts_namespace.layout_wrap_text = {
					
					preWrap : function (widget) {
						jQuery(widget).find('.cat-post-item').each(function(){
							var _that = jQuery(this);
							_that.find('p.cpwp-excerpt-text').addClass('cpwp-wrap-text');
							_that.find('p.cpwp-excerpt-text').closest('div').wrap('<div class="cpwp-wrap-text-stage"></div>');
						});
						return;
					},
					
					add : function(_this){
						var _that = jQuery(_this);
						if (_that.find('p.cpwp-excerpt-text').height() < _that.find('.cat-post-thumbnail').height()) { 
							_that.find('p.cpwp-excerpt-text').closest('.cpwp-wrap-text-stage').removeClass( "cpwp-wrap-text" );
							_that.find('p.cpwp-excerpt-text').addClass( "cpwp-wrap-text" ); 
						}else{ 
							_that.find('p.cpwp-excerpt-text').removeClass( "cpwp-wrap-text" );
							_that.find('p.cpwp-excerpt-text').closest('.cpwp-wrap-text-stage').addClass( "cpwp-wrap-text" ); 
						}
						return;
					},
					
					handleLazyLoading : function(_this) {
						var width = jQuery(_this).find('img').width();
						
						if( 0 !== width ){
							cat_posts_namespace.layout_wrap_text.add(_this);
						} else {
							jQuery(_this).find('img').one("load", function(){
								cat_posts_namespace.layout_wrap_text.add(_this);
							});
						}
						return;
					},
					
					setClass : function (widget) {
						// var _widget = jQuery(widget);

							cat_posts_namespace.layout_wrap_text.handleLazyLoading(this);
						});
						return;
					}
				}
				cat_posts_namespace.layout_img_size = {
					
					replace : function(_this){

						resp_w = _that.width(),
						resp_h = _that.height(),
						orig_w = _that.data('cat-posts-width'),
						orig_h = _that.data('cat-posts-height');
						
						if( resp_w < orig_w ){
							_that.height( resp_w * orig_h / orig_w );
						} else {
							_that.height( '' );
						}
						return;
					},
					
					handleLazyLoading : function(_this) {
						var width = jQuery(_this).width();
						
						if( 0 !== width ){
							cat_posts_namespace.layout_img_size.replace(_this);

							cat_posts_namespace.layout_img_size.handleLazyLoading(this);

					}
				}

				jQuery( document ).ready(function () {
					cat_posts_namespace.layout_wrap_text.setClass(widget);
					
					
						cat_posts_namespace.layout_img_size.setHeight(widget);
					
				});
